). Finally, the Glassey-Strauss decomposition of the derivative of the Maxwell field requires to lose four powers of
v^{0}
, as suggested by Proposition
5.7
and Corollary
5.8
.

Note that the various applications of Proposition
4.11
will not require to control as much moments of
f
than the results mentioned here.

4
Estimates for the distribution function

4.1 Control of the Lorentz force

In view of the structure of the error terms for the commuted Vlasov equations, given by Proposition
2.4
, it is important to obtain precise estimates of the Lorentz force and its derivatives by exploiting its null structure.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
|\gamma|\leq N-2
and
j\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
. For all
(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, we have

\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)}.

If
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
, then we have the improved estimate

\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t+|x|)^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{2}}.

Proof.

Recall the definition of the null components of a
2
-form (
5
) and expand
\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}
according to the null frame
(\underline{L},L,e_{\theta},e_{\varphi})
in order to get

\displaystyle\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\widehat{v}^{L}{F_{L}}^{j}+\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}{F_{\underline{L}}}^{j}+\widehat{v}^{e_{\theta}}{F_{e_{\theta}}}^{j}+\widehat{v}^{e_{\varphi}}{F_{e_{\varphi}}}^{j}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\widehat{v}^{L}(|\alpha(F)|+|\rho(F)|)+\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(|\rho(F)|+|\underline{\alpha}(F)|)+|\widehat{\not{v}}|(|\sigma(F)|+|\alpha(F)|+|\underline{\alpha}(F)|).

(26)

Since
\widehat{v}^{L},\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}},\,|\widehat{\not{v}}|\leq 1
and
|\widehat{\not{v}}|+|v^{0}|^{-1}\leq 2\sqrt{\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}
by Lemma
2.5
, we obtain

\frac{1}{v^{0}}|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}|\lesssim\sqrt{\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}\Big(|\alpha(F)|+|\rho(F)|+|\sigma(F)|\Big)+\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}|\underline{\alpha}(F)|.

(27)

Remark that the same applies to
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
,
|\gamma|\leq N-2
, so that the first estimate ensues from Proposition
3.1
. Assume now that
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
and apply once again (
27
) to
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F
together with Proposition
3.1
. We obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|(t,x)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\log(3+t)\sqrt{\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}(1+|t-|x||)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Lambda\frac{\log^{2}(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{2}},

which implies the result.

If
N-1\leq|\gamma|\leq N
, we do not have improved estimates on the null components of the electromagnetic field. Moreover, if
|\gamma|=N
and
\gamma_{T}=0
, we have a very bad control of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F
near the light cone. The idea then is to transform decay in
t-r
into decay in
t+r
at the cost of powers of
\mathbf{z}
and
v^{0}
.

Lemma 4.2
.

Consider
|\gamma|\leq N
and
j\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
. Then, for all
(t,x,v)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
,

\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F\right|(t,x)\lesssim\Lambda\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}^{2}(t,x,v).

and, if
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
,

\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F\right|(t,x)\lesssim\Lambda\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}^{2}(t,x,v).

Proof.

Recall from Lemma
2.6
that
(1+t+r)^{2}\lesssim(1+|t-r|)^{2}|v^{0}|^{4}\mathbf{z}^{2}
. The first estimate then follows from Proposition
3.2
and the second one from (
BA2
) together with (
25
).

Remark 4.3
.

If
|\gamma|\leq N-1
, we have
|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F|(t,x)\lesssim\Lambda(1+t+|x|)^{-2}|v^{0}|^{2}\mathbf{z}(t,x,v)
. If
|\gamma|\leq N-2
, by combining Lemmas
2.5
and
4.1
, we could even save a power of
|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}
in the first estimate of the Lorentz force and then avoid any loss in
v
.

4.2 Pointwise bounds for
f
and its derivatives

As explained in Section
2.8.2
, the main difficulties here are related to the weak decay rate of the electromagnetic field. We deal with them by exploiting several hierarchies in the commuted equations and by taking advantage of the null structure of the Lorentz force. Our approach, based on the method of characteristics, will require various applications of the following result.

Lemma 4.4
.

Let
g:[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
and
h:[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be two nonnegative sufficiently regular functions such that, for all
(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
,

|\mathbf{T}_{F}(g)|(t,x,v)\leq\frac{C_{g}}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}g+\frac{C_{g}\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{2}(3+|t-|x||)}g+\frac{1}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}h,

for some constant
C_{g}>0
. Then,

\forall\,(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v},\qquad\quad|g|(t,x,v)\leq\Big(\|g(0,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+3\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\Big)e^{6C_{g}}.

Proof.

Fix, for all this proof,
(x,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
and denote by
t\mapsto(X_{t},V_{t})
the characteristic of the operator
\mathbf{T}_{F}=\partial_{t}+\widehat{v}^{i}\partial_{x^{i}}+\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}
satisfying

\forall\;1\leq j\leq 3,\qquad\dot{X}^{j}_{t}=\widehat{V}^{j}_{t},\quad\dot{V}^{j}_{t}=\widehat{V}^{\mu}_{t}{F_{\mu}}^{j}(t,X_{t}),\qquad X_{0}=x,\quad V_{0}=v.

According to Duhamel formula, we have

\forall\,t\in[0,T[,\qquad g\big(t,X_{t},V_{t}\big)=g(0,x,v)+\int_{s=0}^{t}\mathbf{T}_{F}(g)\big(s,X_{s},V_{s}\big)\mathrm{d}s.

We are then lead to introduce the two functions

\psi_{1}(s):=(1+s)^{-1}\log^{-2}(3+s),\qquad\psi_{2}(s):=\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}\big(X_{s}\big)\big(1+\big|s-|X_{s}|\,\big|\big)^{-1}\log^{-2}\big(3+\big|s-|X_{s}|\,\big|\big).

In view of the expression of
\mathbf{T}_{F}(g)
, we have for all
t\in[0,T[
,

g\big(t,X_{t},V_{t}\big)\leq\|g(0,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\int_{s=0}^{t}\psi_{1}(s)\mathrm{d}s+\int_{s=0}^{t}C_{g}\big(\psi_{1}(s)+\psi_{2}(s)\big)g\big(s,X_{s},V_{s}\big)\mathrm{d}s.

Consequently, Grönwall’s inequality and

\int_{s=0}^{+\infty}\psi_{1}(s)\mathrm{d}s=\int_{s=0}^{+\infty}\frac{\mathrm{d}s}{(1+s)\log^{2}(3+s)}\leq\int_{s=0}^{+\infty}\frac{3\,\mathrm{d}s}{(3+s)\log^{2}(3+s)}\leq\frac{3}{\log(3)}\leq 3

yield

\forall\,t\in[0,T[,\qquad\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}g\big(s,X_{s},V_{s}\big)\leq\Big(\|g(0,\cdot,\cdot)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+3\|h\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x,v}}\Big)\exp\left(3C_{g}+C_{g}\int_{s=0}^{t}\psi_{2}(s)\mathrm{d}s\right).

It remains us to estimate the integral of
\psi_{2}
. For this, we will perform a change of variables reflecting that the Vlasov operator reads, in the coordinate system
(u,x,v)
, where
u=t-|x|
,

\mathbf{T}_{F}=\partial_{u}-\widehat{v}^{i}\frac{x_{i}}{|x|}\partial_{u}+\widehat{v}^{i}\partial_{x^{i}}+\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}=2\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}\partial_{u}+\widehat{v}^{i}\partial_{x^{i}}+\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}.

As
\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}>0
by Lemma
2.5
, we can then parameterize
t\mapsto(X_{t},V_{t})
by the variable
u
. Hence, we perform the change of variables
\widetilde{u}(s)=s-|X_{s}|
, so that
\widetilde{u}^{\prime}(s)=2\widehat{V}^{\underline{L}}(X_{s})>0
and

\int_{s=0}^{t}\psi_{2}(s)\mathrm{d}s=\int_{u=t-|x|}^{\widetilde{u}(t)}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2(1+|u|)\log^{2}(3+|u|)}\leq\int_{u\in\mathbb{R}}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{2(1+|u|)\log^{2}(3+|u|)}\leq 3.

We are now able to prove that quantities such as
\mathbf{z}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f
are almost uniformly bounded in phase space. We recall that for a multi-index
\beta
, the number of homogeneous vector fields (respectively translations) composing
\widehat{Z}^{\beta}
is denoted by
\beta_{H}
(respectively
\beta_{T}
).

Proposition 4.5
.

There exists
D>0
, depending only on
(N,N_{v},N_{x})
, such that the following estimates hold. For all
(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
,

\displaystyle\forall\,0\leq q\leq N_{x},\;\;|\beta|\leq N-2,

\displaystyle|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}|\mathbf{z}^{q}\,\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon\,e^{D\Lambda}\log^{3q+3\beta_{H}}(3+t),

(28)

\displaystyle\forall\,0\leq q\leq N_{x}-2,\;\;|\beta|\leq N,

\displaystyle|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}|\mathbf{z}^{q}\,\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\epsilon\,e^{D\Lambda}\log^{3q+3\beta_{H}}(3+t).

(29)

Throughout this paper, it will be convenient to work with
\overline{\epsilon}:=\epsilon\,e^{(D+1)\Lambda}
.

Proof.

For simplicity, we assume here that
N\geq 4
and we sketch the proof of the case
N=3
in Remark
4.6
below. Note further that, by interpolation, it suffices to deal with the cases
q\in\{0,N_{x}\}
for (
28
) and
q\in\{0,N_{x}-2\}
for (
29
). Motivated by the analysis of the toy model carried out in Section
2.8.2
, we introduce the following hierarchised norms in order to deal with non-integrable error terms and still obtain satisfying estimates if the electromagnetic field is large. Consider, for
(N_{0},p,q)=(N-2,N_{v},N_{x})
or
(N,N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2)
,

\mathbb{E}_{N_{0}}^{p,q}[f](t,x,v):=\sum_{|\beta|\leq N_{0}}\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}+\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}|\mathbf{z}^{q}\,\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3q+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

and let us prove that, for all
(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{F}\Big(\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f]\Big)(t,x,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\mathbb{E}[f]_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}(t,x,v)}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(x)\,\mathbb{E}[f]_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}(t,x,v)}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{2}(3+|t-|x||)},

(30)

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{F}\Big(\mathbb{E}_{N}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}[f]\Big)(t,x,v)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\mathbb{E}_{N}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(x)\,\mathbb{E}_{N}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}[f]}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{2}(3+|t-|x||)}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\Lambda\,\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}

(31)

We are able to apply
\mathbf{T}_{F}
to these energy norms since
\mathbf{T}_{F}(|h|)=\mathbf{T}_{F}(h)\frac{h}{|h|}
almost everywhere for any
h\in W^{1,1}_{\mathrm{loc}}
. The result would then follow from two applications of Lemma
4.4
. Fix now
(t,x,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
as well as either
|\beta|\leq N-2
,
p=N_{v}
and
a\in\{0,N_{x}\}
or
|\beta|\leq N
,
p=N_{v}-3
and
a\in\{0,N_{x}-2\}
. Remark then, since
\mathbf{T}_{F}(\log^{-1}(3+t))<0
, that

\displaystyle\mathbf{T}_{F}\left(\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq p\,\mathbf{T}_{F}\big(v^{0}\big)\frac{|v^{0}|^{p-1}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}+a\,\mathbf{T}_{F}(\mathbf{z})\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a-1}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{T}_{F}\big(\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\big)\frac{\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f}{|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}}{{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}}.

(32)

It is important to note that the second term on the right hand side vanishes if
a=0
. We start by dealing with the first two terms on the right hand side since the last one requires a more thorough analysis. As
|\nabla_{v}v^{0}|\leq 1
, we obtain, by applying Lemma
4.1
,

\frac{1}{v^{0}}\big|\mathbf{T}_{F}(v^{0})\big|(t,x,v)=\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}(v^{0})\right|(t,x)\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)},

(33)

so that

\big|\mathbf{T}_{F}\big(v^{0}\big)\big|\frac{|v^{0}|^{p-1}\,|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}\lesssim\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t)(1+|t-|x||)}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}.\color[rgb]{1,1,1}\square\square\color[rgb]{0,0,0}

(34)

Next, recall from (
13
) the identity
\widehat{v}^{\mu}\partial_{x^{\mu}}(\mathbf{z})=0
and remark that
|\nabla_{v}\mathbf{z}|\lesssim\frac{t+r}{v^{0}}
. We get, using Lemma
4.1
,

\left|\mathbf{T}_{F}(\mathbf{z})\right|(t,x,v)\lesssim\sum_{1\leq j\leq 3}\frac{t+|x|}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}(t,x)\right|\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t)}{1+t+|x|}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{1+|t-|x||}.

Using Young inequality for products, we obtain, if
a\neq 0
,

\frac{\mathbf{z}^{a-1}}{\log^{3a}(3+t)}\leq\frac{a-1}{a\log^{3}(3+t)}\frac{\mathbf{z}^{a}}{\log^{3a}(3+t)}+\frac{1}{a\log^{3}(3+t)}.

We then deduce that

\displaystyle a|\mathbf{T}_{F}(\mathbf{z})|\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a-1}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\lesssim\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{3}(3+t)}\right)\!\left(\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}+\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}\right).

(35)

We now focus on the last term in (
32
). The first step consists in applying the commutation formula of Proposition
2.4
and to remark that
v^{0}\partial_{v^{i}}=\widehat{\Omega}_{0i}-t\partial_{x^{i}}-x^{i}\partial_{t}
. We can then bound

\big|\mathbf{T}_{F}\big(\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\big)\big||v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\log^{-3a-3\beta_{H}}(3+t)

by a linear combination of terms of the following form. The good ones, which are strongly decaying and can then be easily handled,

\mathcal{G}^{p,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}:=\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\big|\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)},\quad\qquad|\gamma|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|,\qquad|\kappa|\leq|\beta|-1,

(36)

and the bad ones,

\mathcal{B}^{p,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}:=(t+r)\sup_{1\leq j\leq 3}\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\right|\frac{|v^{0}|^{p}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\big|\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)},\qquad\left\{\begin{array}[]{ll}\gamma_{H}+\kappa_{H}\leq\beta_{H},\\
\kappa_{H}=\beta_{H}\Rightarrow\gamma_{T}\geq 1,\end{array}\right.

(37)

where, again,
|\gamma|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|
and
|\kappa|\leq|\beta|-1
. We emphasize that
\widehat{Z}^{\xi}:=\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}
is composed by the same number of homogeneous vector fields than
\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}
, so that
\xi_{H}=\kappa_{H}
. In contrast,
\widehat{Z}^{\zeta}:=\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}
verifies
\zeta_{H}=\kappa_{H}+1
. Moreover,
\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}
and
\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}
are of order at most
|\beta|
.

Consider first the case
|\beta|\leq N-2
, so that
p=N_{v}
and
a\in\{0,N_{x}\}
, and fix two multi-indices
|\gamma|\leq|\beta|
,
|\kappa|\leq|\beta|-1
. Then, according to Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{N_{v},a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Lambda\left(\frac{\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x|)}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\,\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t)^{\frac{1}{2}}(1+|t-|x|)}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\,\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3(\kappa_{H}+1)}(3+t)}.

(38)

We now focus on
\mathcal{B}^{N_{v},a}_{\gamma,\kappa}
and we start by treating the case
\kappa_{H}=\beta_{H}
and
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
. Applying once again Lemma
4.1
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{N_{v},a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t+|x|)\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t+|x|)^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x|)^{2}}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\,\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+|t-|x|)^{2}}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\,\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\kappa_{H}}(3+t)}.

(39)

Otherwise
\kappa_{H}\leq\beta_{H}-1
, so necessarily
\beta_{H}\geq 1
, and

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{N_{v},a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t+|x|)\left(\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x|)}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}}{(1+|t-|x|)\log^{3}(3+t)}\right)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|}{\log^{3a+3\kappa_{H}}(3+t)}.

(40)

We obtain from (
32
)-(
35
) and (
38
)-(
40
),

\mathbf{T}_{F}\left(\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\,\mathbf{z}^{a}\,|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}\right)\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(x)\,\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(x)\,\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+|t-|x||)^{2}}.

As
|t-r|\gtrsim t
for
r\geq 2t
and
t\geq|t-r|
otherwise, we have

(1+|t-r|)^{-1}\log^{-2}(3+t)\lesssim(1+t)^{-1}\log^{-2}(3+t)+(1+|t-r|)^{-1}\log^{-2}(3+|t-r|)

(41)

and we then deduce that (
30
) holds. Lemma
4.4
then implies (
28
).

Assume now that
N-1\leq|\beta|\leq N
,
p=N_{v}-3
and
a\in\{0,N_{x}-2\}
. We fix two multi-indices
\gamma
,
\kappa
verifying
|\gamma|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|
,
|\kappa|\leq|\beta|-1
and we consider two cases.

Case
1
,
|\gamma|\leq N-2
.
The Lorentz force can still be estimated using Lemma
4.1
. One can then follow the analysis carried out in (
38
)-(
41
) and obtain

\mathcal{G}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa},\;\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\,\mathbb{E}_{N}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}+\frac{\Lambda\,\widehat{v}^{\underline{L}}(x)\,\mathbb{E}_{N}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}[f](t,x,v)}{(1+|t-|x||)\log^{2}(3+|t-|x||)}.

(42)

where the term
\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}
is of course merely defined when
\gamma_{T}
and
\kappa_{H}
satisfy the condition given in (
37
).

Case
2
,
N-1\leq|\gamma|\leq N
.
Then, as
N\geq 4
, we have
|\kappa|\leq 1
so that we will be able to control the terms (
36
)-(
37
) using (
28
). In particular, we are allowed to lose two powers of
|v^{0}|^{2}\mathbf{z}
in the upcoming estimates in order to deal with the weak decay rate of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}F
near the light cone. More precisely, using first Lemma
4.2
and then
a+2\leq N_{x}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}^{2}(t,x,v)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\big|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}}\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3N_{x}+3(\kappa_{H}+1)}(3+t)}.

Next, consider the terms (
37
) and assume first that
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
. In that case,
\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}
can be easily handled since it is strongly decaying. Indeed, using again Lemma
4.2
, we get

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t+|x|)\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}^{2}(t,x,v)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\big|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3N_{x}+3\kappa_{H}}(3+t)}.

Finally, if
\gamma_{T}=0
, we necessarily have
\gamma_{H}=|\gamma|\geq N-1\geq 3
. Since
\beta_{H}\geq\gamma_{H}+\kappa_{H}
, we have
\kappa_{H}\leq\beta_{H}-3
, so that
3a+3\beta_{H}\geq 3(a+2)+3\kappa_{H}+3
. Thus, Lemma
4.2
yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}

\displaystyle\lesssim(t+|x|)\frac{\Lambda\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}|v^{0}|^{3}\mathbf{z}^{2}(t,x,v)\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3a+3\beta_{H}}(3+t)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}\frac{|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\big|\mathbf{z}^{a+2}\partial_{t,x}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\big|(t,x,v)}{\log^{3(a+2)+3\kappa_{H}}(3+t)}.

We then deduce that, in this case,

\mathcal{G}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa},\;\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-3,a}_{\gamma,\kappa}\lesssim\frac{\Lambda}{(1+t)\log^{2}(3+t)}\mathbb{E}_{N-2}^{N_{v},N_{x}}[f](t,x,v).

The estimate (
31
) ensues from (
42
) and this last inequality. To conclude the proof, it then remains to apply again the previous Lemma
4.4
.

Remark 4.6
.

If
N=3
, the proof of Lemma
4.5
requires an additional step. Once the estimate for
\mathbb{E}^{N_{v},N_{x}}_{N-2}[f]
is proved, we need to control the intermediary norm
\mathbb{E}^{N_{v}-1,N_{x}-1}_{N-1}[f]
. For this, compared to the treatment of
\mathbb{E}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}_{N}[f]
carried out during the proof of Proposition
4.5
, there are two differences.

•

First, we can exploit the much stronger decay estimate satisfied by the derivatives of order
N-1
of the electromagnetic field than on its top order ones (see Proposition
3.2
). It explains why we can propagate higher moments for the derivatives of order
N-1
of
f
than for the top order ones.

•

Moreover, for controlling sufficiently well
\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-1,0}_{\gamma,\kappa}
and
\mathcal{B}^{N_{v}-1,N_{x}-1}_{\gamma,\kappa}
in the case
\beta_{H}=\kappa_{H}
, we can prove, through a direct application of Lemma
2.17
, that the good null components of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
still satisfy improved estimates when
|\gamma|=N-1
and
\gamma_{T}\geq 1
.

Finally, in order to bound uniformly in time
\mathbb{E}^{N_{v}-3,N_{x}-2}_{N}[f]
, the analysis of the terms (
36
)-(
37
) is slightly more technical. It is necessary to consider three cases (
|\gamma|\leq N-2
,
|\gamma|=N-1
as well as
|\gamma|=N
) and to use the estimates on the first two energy norms.

4.3 Uniform boundedness of the spatial averages

We start by a preparatory result, which will also be useful later in Section
6
. Recall the constant
\overline{\epsilon}:=\epsilon\,e^{(D+1)\Lambda}
introduced in Proposition
4.5
.

Lemma 4.7
.

For any
|\beta|\leq N-1
, we have

\forall\,(t,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v},\qquad|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-6}\bigg|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x\bigg|\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)}{(1+t)^{2}}.

Proof.

Fix
|\beta|\leq N-1
,
t\in[0,T[
and
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
. Integrating the commutation formula of Proposition
2.4
for
\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f
and performing integration by parts in
x
give

\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x+\sum_{|\gamma|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|}C^{\beta}_{\gamma,\kappa}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x.

Now, we write

v^{0}\partial_{v^{j}}=\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}-x^{j}\partial_{t}-t\partial_{x^{j}}=\widehat{\Omega}_{0j}-(x^{j}-\widehat{v}^{j}t)\partial_{t}-v^{j}S+v^{j}x^{i}\partial_{x^{i}}-t\partial_{x^{j}},\qquad|x^{j}-\widehat{v}^{j}t|\leq\mathbf{z},

so that, integrating once again by parts,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x\right|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}|\gamma|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|+1\\
|\gamma|\leq|\beta|\end{subarray}}\;\sup_{1\leq j\leq 3}\;

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{1}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}(t,x)\right|\left|\mathbf{z}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{t+|x|}{v^{0}}\left|\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\nabla_{t,x}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}(t,x)\right|\left|\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x.

According to the bootstrap assumptions (
BA1
)-(
BA2
) and Lemma
2.6
, we have

|{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}(t,x)|\lesssim\Lambda(1+t+|x|)^{-2}|v^{0}|^{2}\mathbf{z},\qquad|\nabla_{t,x}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}(t,x)|\lesssim\Lambda\log(3+t+|x|)(1+t+|x|)^{-3}|v^{0}|^{4}\mathbf{z}^{2},

so that,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Lambda\sum_{|\kappa|\leq|\beta|+1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}|v^{0}|^{3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{2}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq\Lambda\sup_{|\kappa|\leq|\beta|+1}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\frac{\log(3+t+|x|)}{(1+t+|x|)^{2}}|v^{0}|^{3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-2}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right|(t,x,v)\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-4}(t,x,v)}.

Remark then that, in view of (
13
) and
N_{x}>7
,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-4}(t,x,v)}\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\frac{\mathrm{d}x}{(1+|x-\widehat{v}t|)^{N_{x}-4}}=\int_{y\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{(1+|y|)^{N_{x}-4}}<+\infty.

Then, multiply both sides of the inequality by
|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-6}
and bound above the right hand side by applying Proposition
4.5
. It remains to use
\Lambda\epsilon\,e^{D\Lambda}\leq\epsilon\,e^{(D+1)\Lambda}=\overline{\epsilon}
.

Remark 4.8
.

If
|\beta|\leq N-3
, by using the estimates of the Lorentz force provided by Lemma
4.1
, we can even prove
|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}|\partial_{t}\int_{x}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\mathrm{d}v|\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}(1+t)^{-2}\log^{-3N_{x}-3N}(3+t)
.

Note now that
|\int_{x}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,x,v)\mathrm{d}x|\leq 2\sup_{x}|\mathbf{z}^{4}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(0,x,v)\leq 2\epsilon
. Hence, by integrating in time the inequality of the previous Lemma
4.7
, we obtain, for any
|\beta|\leq N-1
,

\forall\,(t,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v},\qquad\quad|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-6}\bigg|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x\bigg|\lesssim\epsilon+\overline{\epsilon}\int_{\tau=0}^{t}\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+\tau)}{(1+\tau)^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}.

It directly implies the following result.

Corollary 4.9
.

Let
|\beta|\leq N-1
and
\psi:\mathbb{S}^{2}_{\omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a function such that
\|\psi(\cdot,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-6}
. Then, for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,

\forall\,(t,v)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v},\qquad\qquad\bigg|\psi(\omega,v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}x\bigg|\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}.

We allowed the function
\psi
to depend on a parameter
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
in order to prove optimal decay estimates on certain elements of the Glassey-Strauss decomposition of the electromagnetic field, defined as integral kernels.

4.4 Pointwise decay estimates for velocity averages

We prove here that the decay rate of
\int_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\mathrm{d}v
, for
|\beta|\leq N-1
, coincides with the one of the linear setting. In particular, we improve the bootstrap assumption (
BA3
). The starting point consists in performing the change of variables
y=x-t\widehat{v}
. For this, recall Lemma
2.9
and that
y\mapsto\widecheck{y}
is the inverse function of
v\mapsto\widehat{v}
.

Lemma 4.10
.

Let
g:[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a sufficiently regular function. Then,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}g(t,x-\widehat{v}t,v)\mathrm{d}v=\int_{|y-x|<t}(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x-y}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y.

This change of variables is motivated by the linear case. Any regular solution to the relativistic transport equation
\partial_{t}h+\widehat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}h=0
is constant along the timelike straight lines,
h(t,x+\widehat{v}t,v)=h(0,x,v)
. The previous lemma, applied for
g(t,x,v)=h(0,x,v)
, then leads to
\int_{v}h(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim t^{-3}
.

As a first step, we control
\int_{v}|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|\mathrm{d}v
for any
|\beta|\leq N
, which has a slightly slower decay rate than in the linear case in the interior of the light cone. These estimates will also be useful on their own.

Proposition 4.11
.

Let
|\beta|\leq N
and
0\leq a\leq N_{x}-6
. Then, the following properties hold.

•

Almost optimal pointwise decay estimate,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x},\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8}\left|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)}{(1+t)^{3}}.

•

Improved decay estimates near and in the exterior of the light cone,

\displaystyle\forall\,|x|\leq t<T,

\displaystyle\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8-2a}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)\frac{(1+t-|x|)^{a}}{(1+t)^{3+a}},

\displaystyle\forall\,t<\sup(|x|,T),

\displaystyle\qquad\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8-2a}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3+a}}.

Proof.

Fix
|\beta|\leq N
,
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
and
0\leq a\leq N_{x}-6
. If
t\leq 1
, we have by Proposition
4.5
,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-7}\left|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-6}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{w}}\frac{\mathrm{d}w}{\langle w\rangle^{4}}\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}.

Assume now, unless
T\leq 1
, that
t\geq 1
and introduce the function
g(t,x,v):=|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8}|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f|(t,x+t\widehat{v},v)
. Applying the previous Lemma
4.10
to
g
, we get

t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8}\left|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\leq\int_{|y-x|<t}\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{5}g(t,y,v)\mathrm{d}y\leq\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{5}\langle y\rangle^{4}g(t,y,v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{y}}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{\langle y\rangle^{4}}.

Using now Lemma
2.8
and then Proposition
4.5
, we obtain

t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-8}\left|\mathbf{z}^{a}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\leq\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{a+4}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,y,v)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\log^{3a+12+3N}(3+t).

This concludes the proof of the first estimate, which, together with Lemma
2.6
, imply the second one as well as the last one in the region
t<|x|\leq 2t
. If
|x|\geq 2t
, remark that
\mathbf{z}\gtrsim 1+|x-t\widehat{v}|\gtrsim 1+t+|x|
, so that

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-7}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-N_{x}+2}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-2}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,y,v)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{w}}\frac{\mathrm{d}w}{\langle w\rangle^{4}}.

It remains to apply Proposition
4.5
.

Our goal now is to remove the logarithmic loss of the estimate of
\int_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\mathrm{d}v
provided by Proposition
4.11
. Since our analysis will rely on the following result, we will not be able to deal with top order derivatives. We recall that
N_{x}-3>4
.

Lemma 4.12
.

Let
g:[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a sufficiently regular function. Then, for all
|x|<t<T
,

\left|t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}g(t,x-\widehat{v}t,v)\mathrm{d}v-\int_{y\in\mathbb{R}}(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-3}\left(|v^{0}|^{7}|g|+|v^{0}|^{8}|\nabla_{v}g|\right)(t,y,v).

Proof.

According to Lemma
4.10
, we have

t^{3}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}g(t,x-\widehat{v}t,v)\mathrm{d}v-\int_{y\in\mathbb{R}}g\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}v=\mathcal{I}_{1}+\mathcal{I}_{2},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\int_{|x-y|<t}(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x-y}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y-\int_{|x-y|<t}(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y,

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{2}

\displaystyle:=-\int_{|x-y|\geq t}(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y.

Since, by Lemma
2.9
, we have
|\nabla_{y}\widecheck{y}|\lesssim\sqrt{1-|y|^{2}}^{-3}=\langle\widecheck{y}\rangle^{3}=|v^{0}|^{3}(\widecheck{y})
, the mean value theorem gives us

\left|(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x-y}{t}}\right)-(|v^{0}|^{5}g)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{|y|}{t}\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{7}|g|(t,y,v)+|v^{0}|^{8}|\nabla_{v}g|(t,y,v).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{I}_{1}\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-3}\left(|v^{0}|^{7}|g|(t,y,v)+|v^{0}|^{8}|\nabla_{v}g|(t,y,v)\right)\int_{|x-y|<t}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-4}},\qquad N_{x}-4>3.

In order to bound
\mathcal{I}_{2}
recall that
|x|<t
and remark that, for
v=\widecheck{x/t}
and any
y\in\mathbb{R}
such that
|y-x|\geq t
,

\displaystyle 1

\displaystyle=|v^{0}|^{2}\left(1-\frac{|x|^{2}}{t^{2}}\right)\leq|v^{0}|^{2}\frac{|y|(t+|x|)}{t^{2}}\leq 2\frac{|y||v^{0}|^{2}}{t}.

We then finally deduce that

|\mathcal{I}_{2}|\leq\frac{2}{t}\int_{|y-x|\geq t}\left(|v^{0}|^{7}g\right)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\frac{\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-3}}{\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-4}}\mathrm{d}y\leq\frac{4}{t}\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{7}\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-3}|g|(t,y,v).

We are able to prove that the decay of quantities such as
\int_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\mathrm{d}v
is optimal. We state a general result since we will later have to deal with integral kernels.

Proposition 4.13
.

Let
|\beta|\leq N-1
and
\Psi:\mathbb{S}^{2}_{\omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a sufficiently regular function such that
\|\Psi(\cdot,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}+\|v^{0}\nabla_{v}\Psi(\cdot,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-11}
. Then, for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x},\qquad\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Psi(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\frac{\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}.

Proof.

Assume first that
|x|\leq t\leq 1
or
|x|\geq t
. Then, as
|\Psi|(\cdot,v)\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-9}
, it suffices to use the first or the third estimate of Proposition
4.11
, applied for
a=1/2
. Otherwise
t>\max(1,|x|)
and we introduce, for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,
g_{\omega}(t,x,v)=\Psi(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x+t\widehat{v},v)
. Using first Lemma
2.8
and then Proposition
4.5
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}

\displaystyle\langle y\rangle^{N_{x}-3}\left(|v^{0}|^{7}|g_{\omega}|+|v^{0}|^{8}|\nabla_{v}g_{\omega}|\right)(t,y,v)

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\nabla_{v}\Psi|(\omega,v)|v^{0}|^{8}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-3}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,y,v)+\sum_{|\kappa|\leq 1}|\Psi|(\omega,v)|v^{0}|^{7}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-2}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,y,v)

\displaystyle\;\lesssim\sum_{|\xi|\leq N}\,\sup_{(y,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-3}\left|\mathbf{z}^{N_{x}-2}\widehat{Z}^{\xi}f\right|(t,y,v)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t).

(43)

Now, apply Lemma
4.12
to
g_{\omega}
in order to get

\forall\,\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2},\qquad t^{3}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Psi(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{y}}\left(|v^{0}|^{5}g_{\omega}\right)\left(t,y,\widecheck{\frac{x}{t}}\right)\mathrm{d}y\right|+\overline{\epsilon}\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)}{t},

As
t\geq 1
, it remains to bound by
\overline{\epsilon}
the first term on the right hand side. For this, perform the change of variables
z=y-t\widehat{v}
and apply Corollary
4.9
with
\psi(\omega,v)=|v^{0}|^{5}\Psi(\omega,v)
.

The next result is a direct application of the previous Proposition to
\Psi(\omega,v)=v^{\mu}/v^{0}
, for any
0\leq\mu\leq 3
.

Corollary 4.14
.

For any
|\beta|\leq N-1
, the decay of the current density
J(\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f)
is optimal. There exists a constant
C>0
independent of
\epsilon
such that,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x},\qquad\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{v^{\mu}}{v^{0}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\leq\frac{C\,\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}},\qquad 0\leq\mu\leq 3.

If
\epsilon
satisfies
C\overline{\epsilon}=C\epsilon\,e^{(D+1)\Lambda}<C_{\mathrm{boot}}\,\Lambda
, it improves the bootstrap assumption (
BA3
).

4.5 Improved estimates for derivatives of velocity averages

In the linear case, derivatives of averages in
v
, such as
\partial_{t,x}\int_{v}f\mathrm{d}v
, enjoy stronger decay properties. Our study of the top order derivatives of the electromagnetic field will require the following improved estimates.

Proposition 4.15
.

Let
|\beta|\leq N-1
,
\mu\in\llbracket 0,3\rrbracket
and
\Phi:\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
be a sufficiently regular function such that
\|\Phi(\cdot,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}+\|v^{0}\nabla_{v}\Phi(\cdot,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\omega}}\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-10}
. Then, for any
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x},\qquad\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)\partial_{x^{\mu}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\frac{\log^{3N_{x}+3N}(3+t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{4}}.

Proof.

Let
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{x}
and remark that, if
|x|\geq t-1
, the result is given by Proposition
4.11
, applied for
a=1
. We then consider the case
t-|x|\geq 1
. Using (
22
) together with
t\Omega_{ij}=\frac{x^{i}}{r}\Omega_{0j}-\frac{x^{j}}{r}\Omega_{0i}
, one has

\mathcal{I}^{\beta}_{t,x}:=|t-|x||\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)\partial_{x^{\mu}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\leq\sum_{Z\in\mathbb{K}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)Z\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|.

Fix now
Z\in\mathbb{K}
. If
Z
is a translation
\partial_{x^{\mu}}
or if
Z=S
, then
Z\in\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{0}
. Otherwise, either
Z=\Omega_{ij}
is a rotation and
Z=\widehat{Z}-v^{i}\partial_{v^{j}}+v^{j}\partial_{v^{i}}
or
Z=\Omega_{0k}
is a Lorentz boost and
Z=\widehat{Z}-v^{0}\partial_{v^{k}}
, so that

\mathcal{I}^{\beta}_{t,x}\leq\sum_{\widehat{Z}\in\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{0}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|+\sum_{\lambda=0}^{3}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)v^{\lambda}\partial_{v^{k}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|.

Integration by parts and
|\partial_{v^{k}}(\Phi(\omega,v)v^{\lambda})|\leq v^{0}|\nabla_{v}\Phi|(\omega,v)+|\Phi|(\omega,v)\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-10}
yield

\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\Phi(\omega,v)\partial_{x^{\mu}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{|t-|x||}\sum_{|\kappa|\leq 1}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-10}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v.

As
t-|x|\geq 1
, it remains to apply once again Proposition
4.11
for
a=1
.

5
Improvement of the bootstrap assumptions on the electromagnetic field

We are now able to prove pointwise decay estimates for the Maxwell field and its derivatives. We improve first (
BA1
) whereas the case of the top order derivatives (
BA2
) will require a different strategy since we did not recover the linear decay
t^{-3}
for
\int_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v
,
|\beta|=N
.

5.1 The Glassey-Strauss decomposition of the electromagnetic field

We separate
F
as well as its derivatives
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
into three parts according to the Glassey-Strauss decomposition. For this, recall from (
4
) the relation between the electric field
E
, the magnetic field
B
and the Faraday tensor
F
. We have
E^{i}=F_{0i}
,
B^{1}=F_{32}
,
B^{2}=F_{13}
and
B^{3}=F_{21}
. To simplify the statement of the decomposition, we introduce a weight tensor field.

Definition 5.1
.

Let
\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)
be the antisymmetric tensor defined for all
(\omega,v)\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
by

\mathbf{w}_{0i}(\omega,v)=-\mathbf{w}_{i0}(\omega,v):=\omega_{i}+\widehat{v}_{i},\qquad\mathbf{w}_{jk}(\omega,v):=\omega_{j}\widehat{v}_{k}-\omega_{k}\widehat{v}_{j},\qquad 1\leq i,j,k\leq 3,

where
\omega_{i}:=x_{i}/|x|
if
x\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
satisfies
\omega=x/|x|
. We further define

\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v):=\frac{\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}}.

Remark 5.2
.

Since
\mathbf{w}
is antisymmetric,
\mathbf{w}_{\mu\mu}=0
for any
\mu\in\llbracket 0,3\rrbracket
. Note also that
1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}=2v^{L}>0
.

We can now prove an adaptation of Glassey-Strauss result
[
28
, Theorem
3
]
. The key idea of their proof consists in rewriting the standard derivatives
\partial_{t,x}
as combinations of derivatives tangential to a backward light cone, which naturally appears in the representation formula for solutions to wave equations, and
\mathbf{T}_{0}:=\partial_{t}+\widehat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}
, the free relativistic tranport operator which is transverse to light cones. To avoid any confusion with the scaling vector field, we do not keep the notation
S
, used by Glassey-Strauss, in order to denote
\mathbf{T}_{0}
.

Proposition 5.3
.

Let
|\gamma|\leq N-1
. Then, there exists
C^{\gamma}_{\beta},\,N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\in\mathbb{N}
such that

4\pi\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}+\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}+\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S},

where, for any
0\leq\mu,\,\nu\leq 3
and with
\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}
in the following integrals,

•

\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}_{\mu\nu}
can be explicitly computed in terms of the initial data. More precisely,

\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}_{\mu\nu}(t,x)\!=4\pi\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}(t,x)-\!\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}\frac{C^{\gamma}_{\beta}}{t}\!\int_{|y-x|=t}\!\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\!\left(\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\!-\delta_{\mu}^{0}\widehat{v}^{\nu}\!+\!\delta^{0}_{\nu}\widehat{v}^{\mu}\right)\!\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y

and
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}
is the unique solution to the homogeneous wave equation
\Box\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}=0
which initially verifies
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}(0,\cdot)=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}(0,\cdot)
and
\partial_{t}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}(0,\cdot)=\partial_{t}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}(0,\cdot)
.

•

The
2
-form
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}
is given by

\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}_{\mu\nu}(t,x):=-\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\,\big[\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\big]^{T}_{\mu\nu}(t,x),

where the field
[\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f]^{T}
generated by
\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f
is

\big[\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\big]^{T}_{\mu\nu}(t,x):=\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}}.

•

The
2
-form
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}
is defined by

\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}_{\mu\nu}(t,x):=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq|\gamma|}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big(\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\,\widehat{v}^{\lambda}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}\big)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}.

Proof.

Fix
|\gamma|\leq N-1
and apply Proposition
2.4
in order to rewrite the Maxwell equations satisfied by
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
as

\nabla^{\mu}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{v_{\nu}}{v^{0}}f_{\gamma}(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v,\quad\nabla^{\mu}{}^{*}\!\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}=0,\qquad\nu\in\llbracket 0,3\rrbracket,\quad f_{\gamma}:=\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}C_{\beta}^{\gamma}\,\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f,

(44)

with
C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\in\mathbb{N}
. Introduce further the electric
E_{\gamma}
and magnetic
B_{\gamma}
parts of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
,

E^{i}_{\gamma}:=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{0i},\quad i\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket,\qquad B_{\gamma}^{1}=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{32},\qquad B_{\gamma}^{2}=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{13},\qquad B_{\gamma}^{3}=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{21}.

(45)

In such a way, our framework exactly corresponds to the one of Glassey-Strauss. More precisely, one can compute the source terms of the wave equations satisfied by the components of
E_{\gamma}
and
B_{\gamma}
. For any
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
, we have

\displaystyle\quad\Box\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\widehat{v}_{\mu}\partial_{x^{\nu}}f_{\gamma}-\widehat{v}_{\nu}\partial_{x^{\mu}}f_{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v,\qquad\text{so, for instance,}\qquad\Box E^{i}_{\gamma}=-\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\partial_{x^{i}}f_{\gamma}+\widehat{v}_{i}\partial_{t}f_{\gamma}\mathrm{d}v.

(46)

Applying
[
28
, Theorem
3
]
to
(f_{\gamma},E_{\gamma},B_{\gamma})
provides us, for any
0\leq\mu,\,\nu\leq 3
,

4\pi\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}=\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}_{\mu\nu}+\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}_{\mu\nu}-\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\left(\mathbf{T}_{0}f_{\gamma}\right)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

where we recall that
\mathbf{T}_{0}=\widehat{v}^{\lambda}\partial_{x^{\lambda}}
. Note that the constants
C^{\gamma}_{\beta}
in the definitions of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}
,
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}
and
f_{\gamma}
are the same. Applying the commutation formula of Proposition
2.4
for any
|\beta|\leq|\gamma|
yields

\mathbf{T}_{0}f_{\gamma}=-\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\,\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f+C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq|\beta|}C^{\beta}_{\xi,\kappa}\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}\partial_{v^{j}}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f,

(47)

with
C^{\beta}_{\xi,\kappa}\in\mathbb{N}
. It remains to integrate by parts in
v
and to recall that
\nabla_{v^{j}}\cdot\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}={\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{j}}^{j}=0
.

It will then be important to estimate the kernels introduced in the previous Proposition.

Lemma 5.4
.

For all
(\omega,v)\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, we have
|\omega+\widehat{v}|^{2},\,|\omega\wedge\widehat{v}|^{2}\leq 2(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})
and
(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{-1}\leq 2|v^{0}|^{2}
.

Proof.

For the second inequality, simply note that

2|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})\geq 2|v^{0}|^{2}(1-|\widehat{v}|)=2v^{0}(v^{0}-|v|)\geq(v^{0}+|v|)(v^{0}-|v|)=|v^{0}|^{2}-|v|^{2}=1.

For the first ones, since
|\omega|=1
and
|\widehat{v}|\leq 1
,

|\omega+\widehat{v}|^{2}=|\omega|^{2}+|\widehat{v}|^{2}+2\omega\cdot\widehat{v}\leq 2(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}),\quad|\omega\wedge\widehat{v}|^{2}=|\omega|^{2}|\widehat{v}|^{2}-|\omega\cdot\widehat{v}|^{2}\leq(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})(1-\omega\cdot\widehat{v})\leq 2(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}).

Corollary 5.5
.

For any
0\leq\mu,\,\nu\leq 3
and all
(\omega,v)\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, there holds

\left|\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}\right|(\omega,v)\leq 2v^{0},\qquad\quad\frac{\left|\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}\right|(\omega,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})}\leq 4v^{0},\qquad\quad\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}\right|(\omega,v)\leq 6v^{0}.

We have similar bounds for their first order derivatives,

\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}\right|(\omega,v)\lesssim v^{0},\qquad\quad\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})}\right)\right|\lesssim v^{0},\qquad\quad\left|\nabla_{v}\nabla_{v}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}\right|(\omega,v)\lesssim v^{0}.

Proof.

The first two inequalities are a direct consequence of the previous lemma. The other ones ensue from straightforward computations carried out in Lemma
A.2
.

Remark 5.6
.

These bounds are sharp. Let us focus for instance on the first one,
|\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}|(\omega,v)\leq 2v^{0}
. For this, consider, for any
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, the function
\phi_{v}:\omega\mapsto 1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}
defined on
\mathbb{S}^{2}
. Then,

\min_{\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}}\,\phi_{v}(\omega)=\frac{v^{0}-|v|}{v^{0}}=\frac{1}{v^{0}(v^{0}+|v|)}\leq\frac{1}{|v^{0}|^{2}},\qquad\qquad\max_{\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}}\,\phi_{v}(\omega)=\frac{v^{0}+|v|}{v^{0}}\geq 1.

By continuity, there exists
\omega_{v}\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
such that
1+\omega_{v}\cdot\widehat{v}=|v^{0}|^{-2}
. Then, using
|\omega+\widehat{v}|^{2}=2(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})-|v^{0}|^{-2}
, we have

\sum_{1\leq i\leq 3}\left|\mathcal{W}_{0i}\right|^{2}(\omega_{v},v)=\frac{|\omega_{v}+\widehat{v}|^{2}}{|1+\omega_{v}\cdot\widehat{v}|^{2}}=\frac{1}{1+\omega_{v}\cdot\widehat{v}}\left(2-\frac{1}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega_{v}\cdot\widehat{v})}\right)=v^{0}.

In order to improve the bootstrap assumption (
BA2
), we will need to use the Glassey-Strauss decomposition of the spatial derivatives of the electromagnetic field. A similar result holds for the time derivative but we will estimate it by exploiting the Maxwell equations. For instance, one can check that (
2
)-(
3
) imply
|\nabla_{\partial_{t}}F|\lesssim\sum_{1\leq k\leq 3}|\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}F|+|J(f)|
. We lighten the notations by denoting the Lorentz force as

K^{j}:=\widehat{v}^{\mu}{F_{\mu}}^{j},\qquad\qquad K_{\xi}^{j}:=\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j},\qquad 1\leq j\leq 3,\quad 1\leq|\xi|\leq N.

(48)

Proposition 5.7
.

Let
|\gamma|=N-1
and
1\leq k\leq 3
. Then,
\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
can be written as

4\pi\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)=A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}+A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k}+A^{TT}_{\gamma,k}+A^{TS}_{\gamma,k}+A^{SS}_{\gamma,k},

where the five
2
-forms verify the following properties. We fix
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
and we use again the notation
\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}
in the integrals written below. Moreover, we give the definition of the kernels at the end of the statement
9
9

9

We point out that we are only interested in the qualitative properties of these kernels.
.

•

A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}
can be explicitly computed in terms of the initial data,

\displaystyle A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}(t,x)

\displaystyle=4\pi\partial_{x^{k}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}_{\mu\nu}(t,x)-\sum_{|\beta|\leq N-1}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\frac{1}{t^{2}}\int_{|y-x|=t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{D}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{|\beta|\leq N-1}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\frac{1}{t}\int_{|y-x|=t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{C}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\mathbf{T}_{0}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y.

•

A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k}
is the vertex term,

A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}(t,x)=\sum_{|\beta|\leq N-1}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\int_{\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{D}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\sigma,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}.

•

A^{TT}_{\gamma,k}
is the most singular term,

A^{TT}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}(t,x):=\sum_{|\beta|\leq N-1}C^{\gamma}_{\beta}\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{3}}

and the crucial identity
\int_{|\sigma|=1}\mathcal{A}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\sigma,\widehat{v})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}=0
holds for all
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
.

•

A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k}
is given by

A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}(t,x):=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\nabla_{v}\mathcal{B}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\cdot\big(\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\,K_{\xi}\big)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}}.

•

A^{SS}_{\gamma,k}
is the sum of the four following quantities, where
N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\zeta,\kappa}\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle A^{SS,I}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{|\xi|+|\zeta|+|\kappa|\leq N-1}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\zeta,\kappa}\,\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left[\nabla_{v}\big(\nabla_{v}\mathcal{C}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,\cdot)\cdot K_{\xi}\big)\cdot K_{\zeta}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right](t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

\displaystyle A^{SS,II}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\,\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\nabla_{v}\mathcal{C}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\cdot(\mathbf{T}_{0}(K_{\xi})\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

\displaystyle A^{SS,III}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\,\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{C}_{j,\mu\nu}^{k,n}(\omega,v)(\partial_{x^{n}}(K_{\xi}^{j})\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

\displaystyle A^{SS,IV}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\,\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{C}_{j,\mu\nu}^{k,n}(\omega,v)(K_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{x^{n}}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}.

•

The kernels are smooth functions of
(\omega,v)\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
given by

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)

\displaystyle:=-3\frac{\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\omega_{k}}{|v^{0}|^{4}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{4}}-3\frac{\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\widehat{v}_{k}}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{3}}+\frac{\delta_{k\mu}\widehat{v}_{\nu}-\delta_{k\nu}\widehat{v}_{\mu}}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{2}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{B}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)

\displaystyle:=3\frac{\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\omega_{k}}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{3}}-2\frac{\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\widehat{v}_{k}}{(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{2}}-\frac{\delta_{k\mu}\widehat{v}_{\nu}-\delta_{k\nu}\widehat{v}_{\mu}}{1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\omega_{k}\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{2}},\qquad\qquad\mathcal{C}_{j,\mu\nu}^{k,n}(\omega,v):=\mathcal{C}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\frac{\delta_{j}^{n}-\widehat{v}_{j}\widehat{v}^{n}}{v^{0}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\omega_{k}\mathbf{w}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})^{3}}.

Proof.

Let
k\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
,
|\gamma|=N-1
and recall from (
44
) the definition of
f_{\gamma}
and that
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
solves the Maxwell equations with source term
J(f_{\gamma})
. Recall further the electric and magnetic parts
(E_{\gamma},B_{\gamma})
of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
, introduced in (
45
). In the same spirit as in the proof of Proposition
5.3
, we apply
10
10

10

See also the original version of the result,
[
28
, Theorem
4
]
.

[
24
, Theorem
5.4.1
]
to
(f_{\gamma},E_{\gamma},B_{\gamma})
. This yields

\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)_{\mu\nu}=A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}+A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}+A^{TT}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}+\widetilde{A}^{\;T,S}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}+\widetilde{A}^{\,SS}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu},

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}^{\;T,S}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{B}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\big(\mathbf{T}_{0}f_{\gamma}\big)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}},

\displaystyle\widetilde{A}^{\,SS}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}

\displaystyle:=-\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{C}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\big(\mathbf{T}_{0}\mathbf{T}_{0}f_{\gamma}\big)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

as well as
\int_{|\sigma|=1}\mathcal{A}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\sigma,\widehat{v})\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}=0
. One can then prove that
\widetilde{A}^{\;T,S}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}=A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}
by rewriting
\mathbf{T}_{0}f_{\gamma}
using the (commuted) Vlasov equation. More precisely, we use (
47
) and we then integrate by parts in
v
. It remains to deal with
\widetilde{A}^{\,SS}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}
and we recall for this that
\nabla_{v}\cdot K_{\xi}=\nabla_{v^{j}}\cdot\widehat{v}^{\mu}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\mu}}^{j}=0
. Hence, using again (
47
), we get that there exists
N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\mathbf{T}_{0}\mathbf{T}_{0}(f_{\gamma})=\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq|\gamma|}N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\mathbf{T}_{0}\partial_{v^{j}}\left(K_{\xi}^{j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right).

Now, we write
\mathbf{T}_{0}\partial_{v^{j}}=\partial_{v^{j}}\mathbf{T}_{0}-\partial_{v^{j}}(\widehat{v}^{n})\partial_{x^{n}}
and we apply the commutation formula of Proposition
2.4
to
\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f
. We get

\mathbf{T}_{0}\partial_{v^{j}}\left(\widehat{v}^{\lambda}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right)=\partial_{v^{j}}\!\left(\mathbf{T}_{0}(K_{\xi}^{j})\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right)+\partial_{v^{j}}\!\left(K_{\xi}^{j}\mathbf{T}_{0}(\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f)\right)-\frac{\delta_{j}^{n}-\widehat{v}_{j}\widehat{v}^{n}}{v^{0}}\left(\partial_{x^{n}}(K_{\xi}^{j})\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f+K_{\xi}^{j}\partial_{x^{n}}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f\right),

so that, by integration by parts in
v
for the quantities related to the two first terms on the right hand side of the previous equality,

\widetilde{A}^{\,SS}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}=A^{SS,II}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}\!+A^{SS,III}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}\!+A^{SS,IV}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}\!+\!\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq|\gamma|}\!N^{\gamma}_{\xi,\kappa}\!\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\!\nabla_{v^{j}}\!\left(\mathcal{C}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\right)\!(K_{\xi}^{j}\mathbf{T}_{0}(\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f))(\tau_{y},y,v)\frac{\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},

where
\tau_{y}:=t-|y-x|
. Finally, we deal with the last term by applying first the commutation relation of Proposition
2.4
, giving that
\mathbf{T}_{0}(\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f)=-K\cdot\nabla_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f+C^{\kappa}_{\zeta,\beta}K_{\zeta}\cdot\nabla_{v}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f
, and then by integrating by parts in
v
.

These Kernels and their derivatives can be estimated by a direct application of Lemmas
5.4
and
A.2
.

Corollary 5.8
.

For any
1\leq k,\,j,\,n\leq 3
and for all
v\in\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}
, we have

\left(\left|\mathcal{A}^{k}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{A}^{k}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{B}^{k}\right|+\left|\mathcal{C}^{k,n}_{j}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{C}^{k,n}_{j}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{C}^{k}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\nabla_{v}\mathcal{C}^{k}\right|+\left|\mathcal{D}^{k}\right|+\left|\nabla_{v}\mathcal{D}^{k}\right|\right)(\cdot,v)\lesssim|v^{0}|^{3}.

5.2 Three integral bounds

The estimate of most of the terms listed in Propositions
5.3
and
5.7
will in fact be reduced to the analysis of three different integrals. We will deal with all of them by applying a particular case of
[
24
, Lemma
6.5.2
]
.

Lemma 5.9
.

Let
p\in\mathbb{R}
and
g:\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}\rightarrow\mathbb{R}_{+}
be a continuous function. Then, for all
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\setminus\{0\}
,

\int_{|y-x|\leq t}g(t-|y-x|,|y|)\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{p}}=\frac{2\pi}{|x|}\int_{\tau=0}^{t}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}g(\tau,\lambda)\lambda\,\mathrm{d}\lambda\frac{\mathrm{d}\tau}{(t-\tau)^{p-1}}.

The following result will be useful for controlling
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}
and
A^{SS}_{\gamma,k}
.

Lemma 5.10
.

For any
b\geq 4
and for all
(t,x)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}
, there holds

\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{b,1}(t,x):=\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\frac{1}{(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{b}(1+|t-|y-x|-|y||)}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\lesssim\frac{\log(3+|t-|x||)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{b-2}}.

Proof.

Note first that, on the domain of integration,

t-|y-x|+|y|\geq t-|y|-|x|+|y|=t-|x|,\qquad t-|y-x|+|y|\geq|y|\geq|x|-|y-x|\geq|x|-t,

(49)

so that
\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{b,1}(t,x)\leq(1+|t-|x||)^{-b+4}\,\mathbf{Y}^{p=4}_{4,1}(t,x)
and it suffices to treat the case
b=4
. By continuity, we can assume further that
x\neq 0
. According to Lemma
5.9
,

\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{4,1}(t,x)\leq\frac{2\pi}{|x|}\int_{\tau=0}^{t}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}\frac{\mathrm{d}\lambda\mathrm{d}\tau}{(1+\tau+\lambda)^{3}(1+|\tau-\lambda|)}.

We perform the change of variables
\underline{u}=\tau+\lambda
and
u=\tau-\lambda
. Then, on the domain of integration
||x|-t|\leq\underline{u}\leq t+|x|
and
u\leq||x|-t|
. Moreover,
u\geq-\underline{u}
since
2\tau\geq 0
. Consequently,

\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{4,1}(t,x)\leq\frac{\pi}{|x|}\int_{\underline{u}=||x|-t|}^{t+|x|}\int_{u=-\underline{u}}^{||x|-t|}\frac{\mathrm{d}u}{1+|u|}\frac{\mathrm{d}\underline{u}}{(1+\underline{u})^{3}}\leq\frac{2\pi}{|x|}\int_{\underline{u}=||x|-t|}^{t+|x|}\frac{\log(3+\underline{u})}{(1+\underline{u})^{3}}\mathrm{d}\underline{u}.

Now, remark that

\displaystyle\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{4,1}(t,x)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{2\pi\log(3+|t-|x||)}{(1+|t-|x||)|x|}\int_{\underline{u}=||x|-t|}^{t+|x|}\frac{\mathrm{d}\underline{u}}{(1+\underline{u})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\pi\log(3+|t-|x||)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{2}}\frac{t+|x|-|t-|x||}{|x|}

and it remains to note that the last factor on the right hand side is bounded by
2\min(t,|x|)/|x|\leq 2
.

We will apply the next lemma in order to deal with
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}
and
A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k}
.

Lemma 5.11
.

Let, for any
b\geq 3
and all
(t,x)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{b}(t,x):=\int_{|y-x|\leq t}(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{-b}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}}.

Then, the following range of estimates holds. For any
0<\delta\leq 1
,

\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{b}(t,x)\lesssim\delta^{-1}(1+t+|x|)^{-2+\delta}(1+|t-|x||)^{-b-\delta+3},\quad\;\;\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{b}(t,x)\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-2}(1+|t-|x||)^{-b+3}\log(1+t).

Proof.

In view of (
49
), we have
\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{b}(t,x)\leq(1+|t-|x||)^{-b+3}\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{3}(t,x)
and it suffices to treat the case
b=3
. Then remark that

\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{3}(t,x)=\mathbf{K}_{[0,\frac{t}{2}]}+\mathbf{K}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]},\qquad\qquad\mathbf{K}_{I}:=\int_{|y-x|\in I}(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{-3}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}}.

On the domain of integration of
\mathbf{K}_{[0,\frac{t}{2}]}
, we have
t-|y-x|+|y|\gtrsim t+|x|
. Indeed,
t-|y-x|\geq t/2
and
|y|\geq|x|-t
(which controls
|x|/2
if
|x|\geq 2t
). Consequently,

\mathbf{K}_{[0,\frac{t}{2}]}\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-3}\int_{r=0}^{\frac{t}{2}}\mathrm{d}r\leq\frac{1}{2}(1+t+|x|)^{-2}.

(50)

Applying Lemma
5.9
, we have

\mathbf{K}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]}\leq\frac{2\pi}{|x|}\int_{\tau=0}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}\frac{\mathrm{d}\lambda\mathrm{d}\tau}{(1+\tau+\lambda)^{2}(t-\tau)}.

Now, observe that for all
0\leq\tau\leq t/2
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|x|(t-\tau)}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}\frac{\mathrm{d}\lambda}{(1+\tau+\lambda)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\min(|x|,t-\tau)}{|x|(t-\tau)(1+t+|x|)(1+\tau+||x|-t+\tau|)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8}{\max(|x|,t)(1+t+|x|)(1+\tau+|t-|x||)}.

(51)

Let
0\leq\delta\leq 1
and write
(1+\tau+|t-|x||)\geq(1+\tau)^{1-\delta}(1+|t-|x||)^{\delta}
. It remains to integrate in
\tau
in order to derive the expected range of estimates for
\mathbf{K}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]}
.

Finally, a part of our analysis of
A^{TT}_{\gamma,k}
will rely on the following estimate.

Lemma 5.12
.

For all
(t,x)\in[1,+\infty[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\mathbf{Y}^{p=3}_{3}(t,x):=\int_{1\leq|y-x|\leq t}(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{-3}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{3}}\lesssim\frac{\log(t)}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}.

Proof.

The inequality can be easily proved if
t\leq 2
so we assume
t\geq 2
. Start by writing

\mathbf{Y}^{p=3}_{3}(t,x)=\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{[1,\frac{t}{2}]}+\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]},\qquad\qquad\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{I}:=\int_{|y-x|\in I}(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{-3}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{3}}.

Following (
50
), we have

\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{[1,\frac{t}{2}]}\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-3}\int_{r=1}^{\frac{t}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}r}{r}\leq\log\left(\frac{t}{2}\right)(1+t+|x|)^{-3}.

Next, we apply Lemma
5.9
to get

\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]}\leq\frac{2\pi}{|x|}\int_{\tau=0}^{\frac{t}{2}}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}\frac{\mathrm{d}\lambda\mathrm{d}\tau}{(1+\tau+\lambda)^{2}(t-\tau)^{2}}.

If
2t\geq|x|
, we use (
51
) and
t-\tau\geq t/2
in order to derive
\overline{\mathbf{K}}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]}\lesssim t^{-2}(1+t+|x|)^{-1}\log(1+t/2)
, which implies the result. Otherwise,
2t\leq|x|
and we have, for all
0\leq\tau\leq t/2
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|x|(t-\tau)^{2}}\int_{\lambda=||x|-t+\tau|}^{|x|+t-\tau}\frac{\mathrm{d}\lambda\mathrm{d}\tau}{(1+\tau+\lambda)^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\min(|x|,t-\tau)}{|x|(t-\tau)^{2}(1+t+|x|)(1+\tau+||x|-t+\tau|)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2}{|x|(1+t+|x|)(1+|x|-t)(t-\tau)}.

(52)

We get, as
2\leq 2t\leq|x|
,

\displaystyle\mathbf{K}_{[\frac{t}{2},t]}\leq 4\pi\log(2)|x|^{-1}(1+|x|/2)^{-1}(1+t+|x|)^{-1}\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-3}.

5.3 The derivatives of order up to
N-1

In this subsection, we prove pointwise decay estimates for each of the elements of the decomposition of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)
provided by Proposition
5.3
. We start by dealing with
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}
, which is defined on
\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
.

Proposition 5.13
.

There exists
C_{\mathrm{data}}>0
, depending only on
N
, such that

\forall\,|\gamma|\leq N-1,\quad\forall\,(t,x)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\quad\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}\right|(t,x)\leq\Lambda\,C_{\mathrm{data}}(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-1}.

Proof.

In view of the assumptions on the initial data (see Theorem
2.10
) and applying Corollary
5.5
in order to estimate
\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}
, we have for any
|\beta|\leq N-1
,
\omega\in\mathbb{S}^{2}
and
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
,

\displaystyle\forall\,y\in\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\qquad\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\beta}}(F)\right|(0,y)+\langle y\rangle\left|\nabla_{t,x}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\beta}}(F)\right|(0,y)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{|\kappa|\leq|\beta|+1}\langle y\rangle^{|\kappa|}\left|\nabla_{t,x}^{\kappa}F\right|(0,y)\lesssim\Lambda\,\langle y\rangle^{-2},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\left(\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,\widehat{v})-\delta^{0}_{\mu}\widehat{v}^{\nu}+\delta^{0}_{\nu}\widehat{v}^{\mu}\right)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|

\displaystyle\leq 3\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(0,y,v)\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}v}{\langle v\rangle^{N_{v}-1}}\lesssim\epsilon\,\langle y\rangle^{-N_{x}}.

The estimates, at
t=0
, for the time derivatives of the solutions are obtained by using that the equations (
1
)-(
3
) are initially verified. Using (
24
) for
p=N_{x}\geq 3
, we then deduce that

\forall\,(t,x)\in\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{data}}-\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}|(t,x)\lesssim\epsilon(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-N_{x}+1}

(53)

and it remains to use
\epsilon\leq\Lambda
and to apply Proposition
2.21
to
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}
.

Next, we consider
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}
, which is strongly decaying far from the light cone.

Proposition 5.14
.

For any
|\gamma|\leq N-1
, there holds

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda\frac{\log(3+|t-|x||)}{(1+t+|x|)(1+|t-|x||)^{2}}.

Proof.

Fix
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
and recall from Proposition
5.3
the definition of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}
. We have, with
\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}_{\mu\nu}\right|

\displaystyle(t,x)\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq|\gamma|}\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\left|{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}\right|(t-|y-x|,y)\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\widehat{v}^{\lambda}\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}.

Fix now
|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1
,
j\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
and
\lambda\in\llbracket 0,3\rrbracket
. In view of Corollary
5.5
,
\Psi(\omega,v):=\widehat{v}^{\lambda}\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)
satisfies
|\Psi|(\cdot,v)+|v^{0}\nabla_{v}\Psi|(\cdot,v)\lesssim|v^{0}|^{2}\leq|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-11}
. As
|\kappa|\leq N-1
, Proposition
4.13
then gives us

\forall\,(\sigma,\tau,y)\in\mathbb{S}^{2}\times[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\widehat{v}^{\lambda}\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\sigma,v)\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f(\tau,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\frac{\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+\tau+|y|)^{3}}.

Applying this last inequality for
(\sigma,\tau)=(\omega,t-|y-x|)
and estimating the electromagnetic field using the bootstrap assumption (
BA1
), we get

\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\frac{\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda}{(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{4}(1+|t-|y-x|-|y||)}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}=\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda\mathbf{Y}^{p=1}_{4,1}(t,x).

The result then ensues from Lemma
5.10
.

We finally deal with
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}
, which actually enjoys stronger decay properties than
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{S}
for
t\sim|x|
(see Remark
5.16
below).

Proposition 5.15
.

For any
|\gamma|\leq N-1
and all
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, we have

\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}(1+t+|x|)^{-\frac{7}{4}}(1+|t-|x||)^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

Proof.

In view of the definition of
\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}
, introduced in Proposition
5.3
, we have

\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\!\sum_{0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3}\,\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\!\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}},\quad\omega=\frac{y-x}{|y-x|}.

Fix
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
,
|\beta|\leq|\gamma|
and recall from Corollary
5.5
that
\Psi(\sigma,v):=\frac{\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\sigma,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\omega\cdot\widehat{v})}
satisfies
|\Psi|(\cdot,v)+|\nabla_{v}\Psi|(\cdot,v)\lesssim v^{0}
. We then obtain from Proposition
4.13
that

\forall\,\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2},\;\forall\,(\tau,z)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\frac{\mathcal{W}_{\mu\nu}(\sigma,v)}{|v^{0}|^{2}(1+\sigma\cdot\widehat{v})}\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(\tau,z,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\lesssim\frac{\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+\tau+|z|)^{3}}.

Applying this estimate for
\sigma=\omega
,
\tau=t-|y-x|
and
z=y
, we get from Lemma
5.11
that

\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\mathbf{Y}^{p=2}_{3}(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}(1+t+|x|)^{-\frac{7}{4}}(1+|t-|x||)^{-\frac{1}{4}}.

Remark 5.16
.

In fact, Lemma
5.11
also provides
\left|\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{T}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}(1+t+|x|)^{-2}\log(1+t)
. Moreover, the estimate could be significantly improved in the exterior of the light cone, where
|x|\geq t
.

If the constant
C_{\mathrm{boot}}
is chosen such that
C_{\mathrm{boot}}\geq 2C_{\mathrm{data}}
and if
\epsilon
is small enough, Propositions
5.13
,
5.14
and
5.15
allow us to improve the bootstrap assumption (
BA1
).

5.4 The top order derivatives

In this section, we estimate all the terms listed in Proposition
5.7
in order to improve the bootstrap assumption (
BA2
). We start by dealing with the ones depending explicitly on the data.

Proposition 5.17
.

There exists a constant
\overline{C}_{\mathrm{data}}
, depending only on
N
, such that, for any
k\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
and
|\gamma|=N-1
,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\leq\Lambda\,\overline{C}_{\mathrm{data}}(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}.

Proof.

Recall from Propositions
5.3
,
5.7
the expression of
A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}
and from Corollaries
5.5
,
5.8
the bounds on the kernels. Hence, for
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\left|A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\left|\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}\right|(t,x)+\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|+1}\min(t^{-1},t^{-2})\int_{|y-x|=t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{3}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y.

As
[\partial_{x^{\mu}},Z]=0
or
[\partial_{x^{\mu}},Z]=\pm\partial_{x^{\nu}}
for any
Z\in\mathbb{K}
, by the equivalence of the pointwise norms (
9
) and in view of the smallness assumptions on the initial data, there holds

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}\right|(0,y)=\left|\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)\right|(0,y)\lesssim\sum_{1\leq|\kappa|\leq N}\langle y\rangle^{|\kappa|-1}\left|\nabla_{t,x}^{\kappa}F\right|(0,y)\lesssim\Lambda\langle y\rangle^{-3},

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{t,x}\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}\right|(0,y)=\left|\nabla_{t,x}\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)\right|(0,y)\lesssim\sum_{2\leq|\kappa|\leq N+1}\langle y\rangle^{|\kappa|-2}\left|\nabla_{t,x}^{\kappa}F\right|(0,y)\lesssim\Lambda\langle y\rangle^{-4}

As
\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}_{\mu\nu}
is solution to the homogeneous wave equation, Proposition
2.21
gives

\left|\nabla_{\partial_{x^{k}}}\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\gamma}}(F)^{\mathrm{hom}}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\Lambda(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}.

Since
|v^{0}|^{-N_{v}+3}\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v})
, we have for any
|\beta|\leq N
,

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}|v^{0}|^{3}\left|\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f\right|(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\langle y\rangle^{-N_{x}}\sup_{|\kappa|+|\xi|\leq N}\sup_{(x,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{6}}|v^{0}|^{N_{v}+|\xi|}\langle x\rangle^{N_{x}+|\kappa|}\left|\partial_{v}^{\kappa}\partial_{x}^{\xi}f\right|(0,x,v)\lesssim\epsilon\,\langle y\rangle^{-N_{x}}.

Consequently, as
N_{x}\geq 5
, we have

\left|A^{\mathrm{data}}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\Lambda(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}+\epsilon\min(t^{-1},t^{-2})\mathcal{Q}_{t,x},\qquad\mathcal{Q}_{t,x}:=\int_{|y-x|=t}\frac{\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}}{\langle y\rangle^{5}}.

As
\epsilon\leq\Lambda
, it remains to prove
\min(t^{-1},t^{-2})\mathcal{Q}_{t,x}\lesssim(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}
and, for this, we consider two cases.

•

If
t\leq 1
, then
|y|\geq|x|-1
on the domain of integration and
\mathcal{Q}_{t,x}\lesssim 4\pi t^{2}\langle x\rangle^{-5}
. It remains to remark that
\langle x\rangle\geq 1+t+|x|\geq 1+|t-|x||
and
t^{-1}\leq t^{-2}
in the region considered.

•

Otherwise
t\geq 1
and we have
\mathcal{Q}_{t,x}\lesssim t(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}
according to the estimate (
24
). The result ensues from
t^{-2}\leq t^{-1}
in the domain treated here.

Next, we consider the vertex term.

Proposition 5.18
.

Let
k\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
and
|\gamma|=N-1
. We have

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,(1+t+|x|)^{-3}.

Proof.

Fix
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
,
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and recall
N_{v}\geq 15
, so that Corollary
5.8
implies
|\mathcal{D}^{k}_{\mu\nu}|(\omega,v)+|v^{0}\nabla_{v}\mathcal{D}^{k}_{\mu\nu}|(\omega,v)\lesssim|v^{0}|^{N_{v}-11}
. Proposition
4.13
, applied for
\Psi=\mathcal{D}^{k}_{\mu\nu}
and to any
|\beta|\leq N-1
, then yields

|A^{\mathrm{ver}}_{\gamma,k,\,\mu\nu}|(t,x)\lesssim\sum_{|\beta|\leq|\gamma|}\int_{\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\mathcal{D}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\sigma,v)\widehat{Z}^{\beta}f(t,x,v)\mathrm{d}v\right|\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}\int_{\sigma\in\mathbb{S}^{2}}\mathrm{d}\mu_{\mathbb{S}^{2}}=\frac{4\pi\overline{\epsilon}}{(1+t+|x|)^{3}}.

We now estimate
A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k}
. Note that the next result could be easily improved but it is more than enough for the purpose of improving the bootstrap assumption (
BA2
).

Proposition 5.19
.

For any
k\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
and
|\gamma|=N-1
, there holds

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda(1+t+|x|)^{-\frac{3}{2}}(1+|t-|x||)^{-2}.

Proof.

Let
0\leq\mu,\nu\leq 3
,
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and recall that
K_{\xi}^{j}:=\widehat{v}^{\lambda}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}
. Consequently,
|A^{T,S}_{\gamma,k,\mu\nu}|(t,x)
is bounded by a linear combination of terms of the form

\mathcal{Q}^{\xi,\kappa}_{t,x}:=\int_{|y-x|\leq t}\!\left|{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}\right|(t-|y-x|,y)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big|\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{B}_{\mu\nu}^{k}(\omega,v)\big|\left|\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\right|\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}},

with
|\xi|+|\kappa|\leq N-1
and where we recall that
\omega=(y-x)/|y-x|
. Since
|\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{B}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)|\lesssim|v^{0}|^{3}
by Corollary
5.8
and
N_{v}\geq 13
, Proposition
4.11
, applied for
a=1
, provides

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}_{v}}\big|\partial_{v^{j}}\mathcal{B}^{k}_{\mu\nu}(\omega,v)\big|\left|\widehat{Z}^{\kappa}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\right|\mathrm{d}v\lesssim\frac{\overline{\epsilon}\,(1+|t-|y-x|-|y||)}{(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{3+\frac{1}{2}}}.

Moreover, as
|\xi|\leq N-1
, the bootstrap assumption (
BA1
) gives

\left|{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}\right|(\tau,y)\lesssim\Lambda(1+t-|y-x|+|y|)^{-1}(1+|t-|y-x|-|y||)^{-1}.

Consequently, the last two estimates yield

\mathcal{Q}^{\xi,\kappa}_{t,x}\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda\int_{|y-x|\leq t}(1+t-|y-x|)^{-4-\frac{1}{2}}\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|^{2}}=\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda\mathbf{Y}_{4+1/2}^{p=2}(t,x)

and the result follows from Lemma
5.11
.

We pursue with the analysis of
A^{SS}_{\gamma,k}
. As for the previous term, the estimate could be improved.

Proposition 5.20
.

We have, for any
k\in\llbracket 1,3\rrbracket
and
|\gamma|=N-1
,

\forall\,(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3},\qquad\left|A^{SS}_{\gamma,k}\right|(t,x)\lesssim\overline{\epsilon}\,\Lambda\,\langle\Lambda\rangle\,(1+t+|x|)^{-1}(1+|t-|x||)^{-\frac{5}{2}}.

Proof.

We fix
(t,x)\in[0,T[\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
and we recall that
K_{\xi}^{j}:=\widehat{v}^{\lambda}{\mathcal{L}_{Z^{\xi}}(F)_{\lambda}}^{j}
. Recall further from Proposition
5.7
that
A^{SS}_{\gamma,k}
